Sales Payment.jsp SQL:  SELECT a.CHR_SALESNO,a.INT_CUSTOMERID,FIND_A_CUSTOMER_NAME(b.INT_CUSTOMERID),round(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT),a.CHR_PAYMENTSTATUS,  DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_SALESDATE,'%d-%m-%Y') ,  (select ROUND(sum(DOU_PAIDAMOUNT)) from   inv_t_customersalespayment c where c.CHR_SALESORDERNO = a.CHR_SALESNO),  (  round(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT) - (select ROUND(sum(DOU_PAIDAMOUNT)) from   inv_t_customersalespayment c where c.CHR_SALESORDERNO = a.CHR_SALESNO)    ),  a.INT_BRANCHID,a.INT_DIVIID,a.DAT_SALESDATE,    (SELECT   count(*) FROM inv_t_paymentcommitment d WHERE   d.CHR_SALESNO =a.CHR_SALESNO  AND d.CHR_STATUS='N' GROUP BY  d.CHR_SALESNO )  FROM inv_t_directsales a ,inv_m_customerinfo b  WHERE a.INT_CUSTOMERID=b.INT_CUSTOMERID AND a.CHR_CANCEL='N'  AND  a.INT_BRANCHID =1  AND MONTH(a.DAT_SALESDATE) = 8 AND YEAR(a.DAT_SALESDATE) = 2024 ORDER BY a.INT_SALESID

c.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID = b1.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID    AND a.CHR_REF=g.CHR_EMPID    AND e.INT_GROUPID = i.INT_GROUPID   AND j.INT_MODELID = c.CHR_MODEL   AND b.INT_TAXID = k.INT_TAXID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL =  (SELECT  a.CHR_VENDORPO InvNumber, DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_ORDERDATE,'%d-%b-%Y') InvDate, e.CHR_VENDORNAME NameoftheVendor,   e.CHR_GSTNO  VendorGSTNo,   j.CHR_MODELCODE Brand ,  f.CHR_GROUPNAME ProductGroup, c.CHR_DES PCODEDESCRIPTION ,     FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(b.INT_WARRANTY) Warranty,  c.CHR_HSNCODE HSNorSAC ,  FUN_INV_GET_PURCHASE_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS(b.CHR_TYPE,b.CHR_ITEMID, a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO) SlNos,   b.INT_QUANTITY Qty, b.DOU_UNITPRICE UnitCost, ROUND((b.INT_QUANTITY  * b.DOU_UNITPRICE),2) BeforeTax,  b.DOU_TAX_AMOUNT TaxAmt,  b.DOU_TOTAL Total, a.DOU_AMOUNT NetValue,     ROUND(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,2)  FinalValue , k.CHR_TAXNAME GST, g.CHR_STAFFNAME AccountManager , FUN_GET_BRANCH_NAME(a.INT_BRANCHID)  branchname  from inv_t_directpurchase a,inv_t_directpurchaseitem b,inv_m_item c,  inv_m_division d,inv_m_vendorsinformation e,inv_m_itemgroup f, com_m_staff g ,   inv_m_vendorsgroup i, inv_m_model j, inv_m_tax k   WHERE a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO = b.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO    AND  b.CHR_ITEMID =c.CHR_ITEMID   AND b.CHR_TYPE ='I'     AND a.INT_DIVIID= d.INT_DIVIID    AND a.INT_VENDORID = e.INT_VENDORID    AND c.INT_ITEMGROUPID = f.INT_ITEMGROUPID     AND a.CHR_REF=g.CHR_EMPID    AND e.INT_GROUPID = i.INT_GROUPID   AND j.INT_MODELID = c.INT_MODELCODE   AND b.INT_TAXID = k.INT_TAXID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL = 'N'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE >= '2024-08-01'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE <= '2024-08-23'  ORDER BY a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   )   UNION ALL    (SELECT a.CHR_VENDORPO VendorInvoice, DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_ORDERDATE,'%d-%b-%Y') PODate, e.CHR_VENDORNAME NameoftheVendor,   e.CHR_GSTNO  VendorGSTNo,    j.CHR_MODELCODE Brand ,   b1.CHR_NAME ProductGroup, c.CHR_PRODUCTDESC PCODEDESCRIPTION  ,    FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(b.INT_WARRANTY) Warranty,  c.CHR_HSNCODE HSNorSAC ,  FUN_INV_GET_PURCHASE_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS( b.CHR_TYPE,b.CHR_ITEMID,a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO) SlNos,   b.INT_QUANTITY Qty, b.DOU_UNITPRICE UnitCost, ROUND((b.INT_QUANTITY  * b.DOU_UNITPRICE),2) BeforeTax,  b.DOU_TAX_AMOUNT TaxAmt,  b.DOU_TOTAL Total, a.DOU_AMOUNT NetValue,     ROUND(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,2)  FinalValue , k.CHR_TAXNAME GST,g.CHR_STAFFNAME AccountManager  , FUN_GET_BRANCH_NAME(a.INT_BRANCHID)  branchname  from inv_t_directpurchase a,inv_t_directpurchaseitem b,inv_m_produtlist c,  inv_m_productgroup b1, inv_m_division d,inv_m_vendorsinformation e,inv_m_productgroup f, com_m_staff g ,    inv_m_vendorsgroup i, inv_m_model j, inv_m_tax k   WHERE a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO = b.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   AND b.CHR_ITEMID =c.CHR_PRODUCTID   AND b.CHR_TYPE ='P'     AND a.INT_DIVIID= d.INT_DIVIID    AND a.INT_VENDORID = e.INT_VENDORID    AND c.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID = f.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID    AND c.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID = b1.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID    AND a.CHR_REF=g.CHR_EMPID    AND e.INT_GROUPID = i.INT_GROUPID   AND j.INT_MODELID = c.CHR_MODEL   AND b.INT_TAXID = k.INT_TAXID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL = 'N'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE >= '2024-08-01'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE <= '2024-08-23'  ORDER BY a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   )

 (SELECT  a.CHR_VENDORPO InvNumber, DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_ORDERDATE,'%d-%b-%Y') InvDate, e.CHR_VENDORNAME NameoftheVendor,   e.CHR_GSTNO  VendorGSTNo,   j.CHR_MODELCODE Brand ,  f.CHR_GROUPNAME ProductGroup, c.CHR_DES PCODEDESCRIPTION ,     FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(b.INT_WARRANTY) Warranty,  c.CHR_HSNCODE HSNorSAC ,  FUN_INV_GET_PURCHASE_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS(b.CHR_TYPE,b.CHR_ITEMID, a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO) SlNos,   b.INT_QUANTITY Qty, b.DOU_UNITPRICE UnitCost, ROUND((b.INT_QUANTITY  * b.DOU_UNITPRICE),2) BeforeTax,  b.DOU_TAX_AMOUNT TaxAmt,  b.DOU_TOTAL Total, a.DOU_AMOUNT NetValue,     ROUND(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,2)  FinalValue , k.CHR_TAXNAME GST, g.CHR_STAFFNAME AccountManager , FUN_GET_BRANCH_NAME(a.INT_BRANCHID)  branchname  from inv_t_directpurchase a,inv_t_directpurchaseitem b,inv_m_item c,  inv_m_division d,inv_m_vendorsinformation e,inv_m_itemgroup f, com_m_staff g ,   inv_m_vendorsgroup i, inv_m_model j, inv_m_tax k   WHERE a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO = b.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO    AND  b.CHR_ITEMID =c.CHR_ITEMID   AND b.CHR_TYPE ='I'     AND a.INT_DIVIID= d.INT_DIVIID    AND a.INT_VENDORID = e.INT_VENDORID    AND c.INT_ITEMGROUPID = f.INT_ITEMGROUPID     AND a.CHR_REF=g.CHR_EMPID    AND e.INT_GROUPID = i.INT_GROUPID   AND j.INT_MODELID = c.INT_MODELCODE   AND b.INT_TAXID = k.INT_TAXID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL = 'N'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE >= '2024-08-01'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE <= '2024-08-23'  ORDER BY a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   )   UNION ALL    (SELECT a.CHR_VENDORPO VendorInvoice, DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_ORDERDATE,'%d-%b-%Y') PODate, e.CHR_VENDORNAME NameoftheVendor,   e.CHR_GSTNO  VendorGSTNo,    j.CHR_MODELCODE Brand ,   b1.CHR_NAME ProductGroup, c.CHR_PRODUCTDESC PCODEDESCRIPTION  ,    FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(b.INT_WARRANTY) Warranty,  c.CHR_HSNCODE HSNorSAC ,  FUN_INV_GET_PURCHASE_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS( b.CHR_TYPE,b.CHR_ITEMID,a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO) SlNos,   b.INT_QUANTITY Qty, b.DOU_UNITPRICE UnitCost, ROUND((b.INT_QUANTITY  * b.DOU_UNITPRICE),2) BeforeTax,  b.DOU_TAX_AMOUNT TaxAmt,  b.DOU_TOTAL Total, a.DOU_AMOUNT NetValue,     ROUND(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,2)  FinalValue , k.CHR_TAXNAME GST,g.CHR_STAFFNAME AccountManager  , FUN_GET_BRANCH_NAME(a.INT_BRANCHID)  branchname  from inv_t_directpurchase a,inv_t_directpurchaseitem b,inv_m_produtlist c,  inv_m_productgroup b1, inv_m_division d,inv_m_vendorsinformation e,inv_m_productgroup f, com_m_staff g ,    inv_m_vendorsgroup i, inv_m_model j, inv_m_tax k   WHERE a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO = b.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   AND b.CHR_ITEMID =c.CHR_PRODUCTID   AND b.CHR_TYPE ='P'     AND a.INT_DIVIID= d.INT_DIVIID    AND a.INT_VENDORID = e.INT_VENDORID    AND c.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID = f.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID    AND c.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID = b1.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID    AND a.CHR_REF=g.CHR_EMPID    AND e.INT_GROUPID = i.INT_GROUPID   AND j.INT_MODELID = c.CHR_MODEL   AND b.INT_TAXID = k.INT_TAXID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL = 'N'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE >= '2024-08-01'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE <= '2024-08-23'  ORDER BY a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   )

','') NARRATION,   SUBSTRING(c.CHR_DESC, 1, 50) PROD_DEC1 , SUBSTRING(c.CHR_DESC, 51, 50) PROD_DEC2 , SUBSTRING(c.CHR_DESC, 101, 50) PROD_DEC3 , SUBSTRING(c.CHR_DESC, 151, 50) PROD_DEC4 ,    SUBSTRING(c.CHR_DESC, 200, 50) PROD_DEC5 , SUBSTRING(c.CHR_DESC, 201, 50) PROD_DEC6 , SUBSTRING(c.CHR_DESC, 251, 50) PROD_DEC7 ,    FUN_INV_GET_SALES_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS(c.CHR_TYPE,c.CHR_ITEMID, a.CHR_SALESNO) SRNO_DETAILS,    FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(c.INT_WARRANTY) WARRANTY_DETAILS,   a.CHR_OTHERREF PO_REFERENCE, FIND_A_PAYMENT_TERM(a.INT_PAYMENTTERMID) PAYMENT_TERMS, a.CHR_DESPATCHTHRU DESPATCHED_THRU, a.CHR_DESTINATION DESTINATION,   a.CHR_CONTACTDETAILS CONTACT_PERSON, a.CHR_CONTACTNO CONTACT_NO,   CONCAT(FIND_A_CUSTOMER_NAME(a.INT_CUSTOMERID) )CONSIGNEE_NAME, b.CHR_ADDRESS1 CONSIGNEE_ADDRESS1, b.CHR_ADDRESS2 CONSIGNEE_ADDRESS2,   b.CHR_ADDRESS3 CONSIGNEE_ADDRESS3,  g.  (SELECT  a.CHR_VENDORPO InvNumber, DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_ORDERDATE,'%d-%b-%Y') InvDate, e.CHR_VENDORNAME NameoftheVendor,   e.CHR_GSTNO  VendorGSTNo,   j.CHR_MODELCODE Brand ,  f.CHR_GROUPNAME ProductGroup, c.CHR_DES PCODEDESCRIPTION ,     FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(b.INT_WARRANTY) Warranty,  c.CHR_HSNCODE HSNorSAC ,  FUN_INV_GET_PURCHASE_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS(b.CHR_TYPE,b.CHR_ITEMID, a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO) SlNos,   b.INT_QUANTITY Qty, b.DOU_UNITPRICE UnitCost, ROUND((b.INT_QUANTITY  * b.DOU_UNITPRICE),2) BeforeTax,  b.DOU_TAX_AMOUNT TaxAmt,  b.DOU_TOTAL Total, a.DOU_AMOUNT NetValue,     ROUND(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,2)  FinalValue , k.CHR_TAXNAME GST, g.CHR_STAFFNAME AccountManager , FUN_GET_BRANCH_NAME(a.INT_BRANCHID)  branchname  from inv_t_directpurchase a,inv_t_directpurchaseitem b,inv_m_item c,  inv_m_division d,inv_m_vendorsinformation e,inv_m_itemgroup f, com_m_staff g ,   inv_m_vendorsgroup i, inv_m_model j, inv_m_tax k   WHERE a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO = b.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO    AND  b.CHR_ITEMID =c.CHR_ITEMID   AND b.CHR_TYPE ='I'     AND a.INT_DIVIID= d.INT_DIVIID    AND a.INT_VENDORID = e.INT_VENDORID    AND c.INT_ITEMGROUPID = f.INT_ITEMGROUPID     AND a.CHR_REF=g.CHR_EMPID    AND e.INT_GROUPID = i.INT_GROUPID   AND j.INT_MODELID = c.INT_MODELCODE   AND b.INT_TAXID = k.INT_TAXID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL = 'N'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE >= '2024-08-01'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE <= '2024-08-23'  ORDER BY a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   )   UNION ALL    (SELECT a.CHR_VENDORPO VendorInvoice, DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_ORDERDATE,'%d-%b-%Y') PODate, e.CHR_VENDORNAME NameoftheVendor,   e.CHR_GSTNO  VendorGSTNo,    j.CHR_MODELCODE Brand ,   b1.CHR_NAME ProductGroup, c.CHR_PRODUCTDESC PCODEDESCRIPTION  ,    FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(b.INT_WARRANTY) Warranty,  c.CHR_HSNCODE HSNorSAC ,  FUN_INV_GET_PURCHASE_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS( b.CHR_TYPE,b.CHR_ITEMID,a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO) SlNos,   b.INT_QUANTITY Qty, b.DOU_UNITPRICE UnitCost, ROUND((b.INT_QUANTITY  * b.DOU_UNITPRICE),2) BeforeTax,  b.DOU_TAX_AMOUNT TaxAmt,  b.DOU_TOTAL Total, a.DOU_AMOUNT NetValue,     ROUND(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,2)  FinalValue , k.CHR_TAXNAME GST,g.CHR_STAFFNAME AccountManager  , FUN_GET_BRANCH_NAME(a.INT_BRANCHID)  branchname  from inv_t_directpurchase a,inv_t_directpurchaseitem b,inv_m_produtlist c,  inv_m_productgroup b1, inv_m_division d,inv_m_vendorsinformation e,inv_m_productgroup f, com_m_staff g ,    inv_m_vendorsgroup i, inv_m_model j, inv_m_tax k   WHERE a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO = b.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   AND b.CHR_ITEMID =c.CHR_PRODUCTID   AND b.CHR_TYPE ='P'     AND a.INT_DIVIID= d.INT_DIVIID    AND a.INT_VENDORID = e.INT_VENDORID    AND c.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID = f.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID    AND c.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID = b1.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID    AND a.CHR_REF=g.CHR_EMPID    AND e.INT_GROUPID = i.INT_GROUPID   AND j.INT_MODELID = c.CHR_MODEL   AND b.INT_TAXID = k.INT_TAXID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL = 'N'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE >= '2024-08-01'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE <= '2024-08-23'  ORDER BY a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   )

 (SELECT  a.CHR_VENDORPO InvNumber, DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_ORDERDATE,'%d-%b-%Y') InvDate, e.CHR_VENDORNAME NameoftheVendor,   e.CHR_GSTNO  VendorGSTNo,   j.CHR_MODELCODE Brand ,  f.CHR_GROUPNAME ProductGroup, c.CHR_DES PCODEDESCRIPTION ,     FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(b.INT_WARRANTY) Warranty,  c.CHR_HSNCODE HSNorSAC ,  FUN_INV_GET_PURCHASE_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS(b.CHR_TYPE,b.CHR_ITEMID, a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO) SlNos,   b.INT_QUANTITY Qty, b.DOU_UNITPRICE UnitCost, ROUND((b.INT_QUANTITY  * b.DOU_UNITPRICE),2) BeforeTax,  b.DOU_TAX_AMOUNT TaxAmt,  b.DOU_TOTAL Total, a.DOU_AMOUNT NetValue,     ROUND(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,2)  FinalValue , k.CHR_TAXNAME GST, g.CHR_STAFFNAME AccountManager , FUN_GET_BRANCH_NAME(a.INT_BRANCHID)  branchname  from inv_t_directpurchase a,inv_t_directpurchaseitem b,inv_m_item c,  inv_m_division d,inv_m_vendorsinformation e,inv_m_itemgroup f, com_m_staff g ,   inv_m_vendorsgroup i, inv_m_model j, inv_m_tax k   WHERE a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO = b.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO    AND  b.CHR_ITEMID =c.CHR_ITEMID   AND b.CHR_TYPE ='I'     AND a.INT_DIVIID= d.INT_DIVIID    AND a.INT_VENDORID = e.INT_VENDORID    AND c.INT_ITEMGROUPID = f.INT_ITEMGROUPID     AND a.CHR_REF=g.CHR_EMPID    AND e.INT_GROUPID = i.INT_GROUPID   AND j.INT_MODELID = c.INT_MODELCODE   AND b.INT_TAXID = k.INT_TAXID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL = 'N'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE >= '2024-08-01'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE <= '2024-08-23'  ORDER BY a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   )   UNION ALL    (SELECT a.CHR_VENDORPO VendorInvoice, DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_ORDERDATE,'%d-%b-%Y') PODate, e.CHR_VENDORNAME NameoftheVendor,   e.CHR_GSTNO  VendorGSTNo,    j.CHR_MODELCODE Brand ,   b1.CHR_NAME ProductGroup, c.CHR_PRODUCTDESC PCODEDESCRIPTION  ,    FUN_INV_GET_WARRANTY_IN_YEAR(b.INT_WARRANTY) Warranty,  c.CHR_HSNCODE HSNorSAC ,  FUN_INV_GET_PURCHASE_SERIALNUMBER_CONCATS( b.CHR_TYPE,b.CHR_ITEMID,a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO) SlNos,   b.INT_QUANTITY Qty, b.DOU_UNITPRICE UnitCost, ROUND((b.INT_QUANTITY  * b.DOU_UNITPRICE),2) BeforeTax,  b.DOU_TAX_AMOUNT TaxAmt,  b.DOU_TOTAL Total, a.DOU_AMOUNT NetValue,     ROUND(a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,2)  FinalValue , k.CHR_TAXNAME GST,g.CHR_STAFFNAME AccountManager  , FUN_GET_BRANCH_NAME(a.INT_BRANCHID)  branchname  from inv_t_directpurchase a,inv_t_directpurchaseitem b,inv_m_produtlist c,  inv_m_productgroup b1, inv_m_division d,inv_m_vendorsinformation e,inv_m_productgroup f, com_m_staff g ,    inv_m_vendorsgroup i, inv_m_model j, inv_m_tax k   WHERE a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO = b.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   AND b.CHR_ITEMID =c.CHR_PRODUCTID   AND b.CHR_TYPE ='P'     AND a.INT_DIVIID= d.INT_DIVIID    AND a.INT_VENDORID = e.INT_VENDORID    AND c.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID = f.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID    AND c.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID = b1.INT_PRODUCTGROUPID    AND a.CHR_REF=g.CHR_EMPID    AND e.INT_GROUPID = i.INT_GROUPID   AND j.INT_MODELID = c.CHR_MODEL   AND b.INT_TAXID = k.INT_TAXID   AND a.CHR_CANCEL = 'N'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE >= '2024-08-01'  AND a.DAT_ORDERDATE <= '2024-08-23'  ORDER BY a.CHR_PURCHASEORDERNO   )
